4515: function isoDate(d){
4516: let y=d.getFullYear(),m=String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0"),day=String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");
4517: return `${y}-${m}-${day}`;
4518: }
4519: function mondayOf(d){
4520: let x=new Date(d); x.setHours(12,0,0,0);
4521: let day=x.getDay(); let diff=day===0?-6:1-day; x.setDate(x.getDate()+diff); return x;
4522: }
4523: function weekValue(d){
4524: let x=mondayOf(d), jan4=new Date(x.getFullYear(),0,4,12), first=mondayOf(jan4);
4525: let week=Math.round((x-first)/604800000)+1;
4526: return `${x.getFullYear()}-W${String(week).padStart(2,"0")}`;
4527: }
4528: function mondayFromWeek(v){
4529: let [y,w]=v.split("-W").map(Number); if(!y||!w)return mondayOf(new Date());
4530: let jan4=new Date(y,0,4,12), first=mondayOf(jan4), d=new Date(first);d.setDate(d.getDate()+(w-1)*7);return d;
4531: }
